1b1('bum): @##/ O /shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa stong phrag brgya pa/dum bu lnga pa/bam po dang po'o//
1b2('bum): tshe dang ldan pa sha ra da ti'i bu gzug+sa med pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' sngon gyi mthar mi dmigaso༐
1b3('bum): gzugs stong pa nyid kyi phyir byang chub sems dpa'-i sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs so//gzugs 
1b4('bum): dben pa'i phyir byang chub sems pa'-i sngon gyi mthar mi dmi gas so//gzugs ngo po nyid med 
1b5('bum): pa'i phyir byang chub sems dba' sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs so//gzugs med pa'i phyir byang chub 
1b6('bum): sems dpa'   phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs so//gzugs stong pa nyid kyi phyirabyang chub sems 
2a1('bum): @#/ O /dpa' phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs so//gzugs dben pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' phyi ma'i mthar 
2a2('bum): mi dmigs so//gzugs ngo bo nyid med pa'i phyir byang chub sems pa' phyi ma'i mthar mi dmig so//gzugs med 
2a3('bum): pa'i phyir byang chub sems dba' dbus su mi dmigaso//gzugs stong pa nyid kyi phyir byang chub sems dpa'i dbus su 
2a4('bum): mi dmigs so//gzug sa dben pa'i phyir byang cub sems dpa' dbus su mi dmigs so//gzugs ngobo nyid med 
2a5('bum): pa'i phyir byang chub sams dpa' dbus su mi da migs so//de ci'i phyir zhe na/tshe dang ldan pa sha ra dwa ti'i bu gzugs med 
2a6('bum): pa dang /gzugs stong pa nyid dang /gzugs dben pa dang /gzugs ngo bo nyid med pa la sngon gyi mtha' mi dmigs//
2b1('bum): phyi ma'i mtha' mi dmigs/dbus mi dmigs te/gzugs med pa 'ang gzhan ma yin/gzugs stong pa nyid kyang gzhan ma yin/gzugs dben 
2b2('bum): pa 'ang gzhan ma yin/gzungs ngo bo nyid med pa 'ang gzhan ma yin /byang chub sems dpa' 'ang gzhan ma yin/sngon gyi mtha' 'ang gzhan ma yin/phyi ma'i 
2b3('bum): mtha' 'ang gzhan ma yin/dbus kyang gzhan ma yin no//tshe dang ldan pa sha ra dwa ti'i bu de ltar na gzugs med pa gang yin pa dang /gzugs stong pa nyid gang 
2b4('bum): yin pa dang /gzugs dben pa gang yin pa dang /gzags ngo bo nyid med pa gang yin pa dang/byang chub sems dpa' gang yin pa dang /sngon gyi 
2b5('bum): mtha' gang yin pa dang /phyi ma'i mtha' gang yin pa dang /dbus gang yin pa de thams cad kyang gnyis su med de gnyis su byar med do//tshe dang ldan 
2b6('bum): pa sha ra dwa ti'i bu sgra med pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs so//sgra stong pa nyid kyi phyir byang chub sems dpa'-i sngon gyi mthar mi 
2b7('bum): dmigaso//sgra dben pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' sngon gyi mthar mi dmi gso//sgra ngo bo nyid med pI phyir byang chub sems dbA sngon gyi mthar mi dmigaso//sgra med pa'i 
3a1('bum): phyir byang chub sems dpa' phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs so//sgra stong pa nyid kyi phyir byang chub sems dpa'-i phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs  so//sgra 
3a2('bum): dben pa'i phyir byang chub sems dang 'a phyi ma'i mthar mi dmig so//sgra ngo bo nyid med pa'i phyir byang chub sems pa' phyi ma'i mthar mi dmi gas so//sgra med pa'i phyir byang chub 
3a3('bum): sems dpa' dbus su mi dmigs so//sgra stongapa nyid kyi phyir byang chub ches dpa' dbus su mi dmi gas so//sgra dben pa'i phyir byang chub
3a4('bum): nyid med pa'i phyir byang chu sems dpa' dbus su mi gas so//de ci'i phyir zhe na/tshe dang ldan pa sha ra dwa ti'i bu sgra med pa dang /sgra stong pa nyid dang /sgra dben pa dang /sgra ngo bo
3a5('bum): nyid med pa la sngon gyi mtha' mi dmigs phyi ma'i mtha' mi dmigs/dbus mi dmigs te/sgra med pa 'ang gzhan ma yin/sgra stong pa nyid kyang gzhan ma yin no//sgra dben
3a6('bum): pa 'ang gzhan ma yin//sgra ngo bo nyid med pa 'ang gzhan ma yi/byang chub sems dpa' 'ang gzhan ma yin/sngon gyi mtha' 'ang gzhan ma yin/phyi ma'i mtha' 'ang gzhan yin/
3a7('bum): dbus kyang gzhan ma yin no//tshe dang ldan pa sha ra dwati'i bu de ltar na sgra med pa 'ang gzhan ma yI/sgra stong pa nyid gang yin pa dang /sgra dben pa gang yin pa dang /sgra ngo bo nyid med
3b1('bum): pa gang yin pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' gang yin pa dang /sngon gyi mtha' gang yin pa dang /phyi ma'i mtha' gang yin pa dang dbus gang yin pa de thams cad kyang gnyis su med
3b2('bum): de gnyis su byar med do//tshe dang ldan pa sha ra dwa ti'i bu dri med pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs so//dri stong pa nyid kyi phyir byang chub sems dpa'i sngon gyi mthar
3b3('bum): mi dmigs so//dri dben pa'i phyir byang chub sems pa'i sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs swo//dri ngo bo nyid med pa'i phyir byang chub sesams dpa' sngon gyi mthar 
3b4('bum): phyir byang chub sems dpa'-i phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs so//dri stong pa nyid kyi phyir bya chub sems dpa' phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs
3b5('bum): mthar mi dmigs so//dri+i ngo bo nyid med pa'i phyir yang chub sems dpa' phyi ma'i thar mi dmigs so//dri med pa'i phyir byang chub
3b6('bum): nyid kyi phyir byang chub sem dpa' dbus su mi dmigs so//dri dbe pa'i phyir byang chub sems
3b7('bum): su mi dmigs so//de ci'i phyir zhe na/tshe dang ldan pa sha ra dA ti'i bu dri med pa dang /dri stong pa nyid dang /dri dben pa dang /dri ngo bo nyid med pa la sngon gyi mtha' mi dmigs/phyi
4a1('bum): @#/ O /ma'i mtha' mi dmigs/dbus mi dmigs te/dri med pa 'ang gzhan ma yin/dri stong pa nyid kyang gzhan ma yin/dri dben pa 'ang gzhan ma yin/
4a2('bum): dri ngo bo nyid med pa 'ang gzhan ma yin /byang chub sems dpa' 'ang gzhan ma yin/sngon gyi mtha' 'ang gzhan ma yin/phyi ma'i mtha' 'ang gzhan ma yin /dbus kyang gzhan ma 
4a3('bum): yino༐ ༐tshe dang ldan pa sha ra dwa ti'i bu de ltar na dri med pa gang yin pa dang /dri stong pa nyid gang yin pa dang /dri dben pa gang yin pa dang //dri ngo bo nyid med pa gang yin pa dang/
4a4('bum): byang chub sems dpa' gang yin pa dang /sngon gyi mtha' gang yin pa dang /phyi ma'i mtha' gang yin pa dang/dbus gang yin pa de thams cad kyang gnyis su med de gnyis su 
4a5('bum): byar med do//tshe dang ldan pa sha ra dA ti'i bu ro med pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs so//ro stong pa nyid kyi phyir byang chub sems dpa'e sngon gyi mthar mi 
4a6('bum): dmigs so//ro dben pa'i phyir byang chub sems pa' sngon gyi mthar mi dmig so//ro ngo bo nyid med pa'i phyir byang chub ses dpa' sngon gyi mthar midamigs so//ro med pa'i
4a7('bum): phyir byang chub sems dpa'aphyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs so//ro stong pa nyid kyi phyir byangachub sems dpa' phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs so//ro dben pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' phyi 
4b1('bum): ma'i mthar mi dmigs so//ro ngo bo nyid med pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs so//ro med pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' dbus su mi dmigs so//ro stong pa nyid kyi
4b2('bum): phyir byang chub sems dpa'-i dbus su mi dmigs so//ro dben pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' dbus su mi dmigs so//ro ngo bo nyid med pa'i phyir byang chub sems pa'-i dbus su mi dmigs
4b3('bum): so||de ci'i phyir zhe na/tshe dang ldan pa sha ra dwa ti'i bu ro med pa dang /ro stong pa nyid dang /ro dben pa dang /ro ngo bo nyid med pa la sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs sa//phyi ma'i mtha'
4b4('bum): mi dmigs/dbus mi dmigs te/ro med pa'ang gzhan ma yin/ro stong pa nyid kyang gzhan ma yin/ro dben pa'ang gzhan ma yin//ro ngo bo nyid med pa 'ang gzhan ma yin/byang
4b5('bum): chub sems dpa' 'ang gzhan ma yin /sngon gyi mtha' 'ang gzhan ma yin/phyi ma'i mtha' 'ang gzhan ma yin
4b6('bum): yin pa dang /ro stong pa nying gang yin pa dang /ro dben pa gang yin pa dang /ro ngo bo nyid med pa gang yin pa dang /
4b7('bum): byang chub sems dpa' gang yin pa dang /sngon gyi mtha' gang yin pa
4b8('bum): dang |phyi ma'i mtha' gang yin pa dang /dbus gang yin pa de thams cad kyang gnyis su med de gnyis su byar med do//tshe dang ldan pa sha ra dwa ti'i bu reg med pa'i phyir/byang chub
5a1('bum): sems dpa' sngon gyi mtha' mi dmigs so//reg stong pa nyid kyi phyir byang chub sems dpa' sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs so//reg dben pa'i 
5a2('bum): phyir byang chub sems dpa' sngaun gyi mthar mi dmigs so //reg ngo bo nyid med pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa'i sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs swo//reg med pa'i phyir byang chub 
5a3('bum): sems dpa' phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs so//rag stong pa nyid kyi phyir byam chub sems dpa' phyi ma'i mthar ma dmigs so//reg dbe pa'i phyir byang chub sems
5a4('bum): dmigs so//reg ngo bo nyid med ba'i phyir byang chub sems dba' phyi ma'i mthar mi dmig swo//reg med pa'i byang chub sems dpa' dbus su mi dmigs so//reg 
5a5('bum): nyid kyi phyir byang chub sem dpa' dbus su mi dmig swo//reg dben pa'i phyir byang chub sems 
5a6('bum): dbus su mi dmigs swo//de ci'i phyir zhe na/tshe dang ldan pa sha ra dwa ti'i bu reg med pa dang /reg stong pa nyid dang /reg dben pa dang /reg ngo bo nyid med pa la sngon gyi mtha' 
5a7('bum): mi dmigs/phyi ma'i mtha' mi dmigs/dbus mi dmigs te/reg med pa 'ang gzhan ma yin/reg stong pa nyid kyang gzhan ma yin/reg dben pa 'ang gzhan ma yin /reg ngo bo
5b1('bum): nyid med pa 'ang gzhan ma yin/byang chub sems dpa' 'ang gzhan ma yin/sngon gyi mtha' 'ang gzhan ma yin/phyi ma'i mtha' 'ang gzhan ma yin /dbus kyang gzhan ma yin no//
5b2('bum): tshe dang ldan pa sha ra dwa ti'i bu de ltar na reg pa med pa gang yin pa dang /reg stong pa nyid gang yin pa dang /regadaben pa gang yin pa dang /rig ngo bo nyid med pa gang yin pa dang/byang 
5b3('bum): chub sems dpa' gang yin pa dang /sngon gyi mtha' gang yin pa dang /phyi ma'i mtha' gang yin pa dang/dbus gang yin pa de thams cad kyang gnyis su med de gnyis su byar mdo//
5b4('bum): tshe dang ldan pa sha ra ng+wa ti'i bu chos med pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs so//chos stong pa nyid kyi phyir byang chub sems dpa'i sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs
5b5('bum): chos dben pa'i phyir byang chub sems pa' sngon gyi mthar mi dmig so//chos ngo bo nyid med pa'i phyir byang chub sesam dpa' sngon gyi mthar mi 'damigs so//chos med pa'i
5b6('bum): phyir byang chub sems dpa'-i phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs so/chos stong pa nyid kyi phyir byung chub sems dpa' phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs
5b7('bum): phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs swo//chos ngo bo nyid med 'i phyir byang chub sems dang 'a phyi ma'i mthar mi dmig swo//chos med pa'i phyir byang chu ba sems dpa' dbus su mi dmigs so//
6a1('bum): @#/ O /chos stong pa nyid kyi phyir byang chub sems dpa' dbus su mi dmigs so//chos dben pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa'-i dbus su mi dmigs so/ /chos ngo bo
6a2('bum): nyid med pa'i phyir byang chub sems pa' dbus su mi dmigs so //de ci'i phyir zhe na/tshe dang ldan pa sha ra dwa ti'i bu chos med pa dang /chos stong pa nyid dang /chos dben pa dang /
6a3('bum): chos ngo bo nyid med pa la sngon gyi mtha' mi dmigs //phyi ma'i mtha' mi dmigs/dbus mi dmigs te/chos med pa 'ang gzhan ma yin/chos stong pa nyid kyang gzhan ma yin/
6a4('bum): chos dben pa nyid kyang gzhan ma yin /chos ngo bo nyid med pa 'ang gzhan ma yin /byang chub sems dpa' 'ang gzhan ma yin/sngon gyi mtha' 'ang gzhan ma yin /phyi ma'i mtha' 'ang 
6a5('bum): gzhan ma yin/dbus kyang gzhan ma yin no//tshe dang ldan pa sha ra dwati'i bu de ltar na chos med pa gang yin pa dang /chos stong pa nying gang yin pa dang /chos dben pa gang yin pa 
6a6('bum): dang /chos ngo bo nyid med pagang yin pa dang /byang chub sems dpa'-i gang yin pa dang /sngon gyi mtha' gang yin pa dang /phyi ma'i mtha' gang yin pa dang dbus gang yin pa de 
6a7('bum): thams cad kyang gnyis su med de gnyis su byar med do//tshe dang ldan pa sha ra b+wa ti'i bu migigi rnam par shes pa med pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs so//mig gi 
6b1('bum): rnam par shes pa stong pa nyid kyi phyir byang chub sems dpa' sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs so//mig gi rnam par shes pa dben pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa'i sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs sa so//
6b2('bum): mig gi rnam par shes pa ngo bo nyid med pa'i phyir byang chub sems pa dpa' sngon gyi mthar mi 'di dmigs so//mig gi rnal par shes pa med pa'i phyir byang chub ces sems dpa' phyi ma'i 
6b3('bum): mthar mi dmigs so//migag gi rnam par shes pa stong pa gnyid kyi phyir byang chub sem dpa' phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs so//mig gi rnam par shes pa deben pa'i phyir byang 
6b4('bum): chub sems dpa' phyi ma'i mthar mi 'di dmigs sa so//mig gi rnam pa shes pa ngo bo nyid med dmigs so//mig gi dmigs 
6b5('bum): par shes pa med pa'i phyir bar chub sems dpa' dbus su mi dmigs so//mig gis rnam par shes pa stong nyid kyi phyir byang chub sel dpa' dbus su mi dmig sa so//
6b6('bum): mig gi rnam bar shes pa dben pa'i phyi byang chub sems dpa' dbus gsu mi dmigs so//mig ni rnam par shes pa ngo bo gnyid med pa'i phyir byang cub sems dpa' dbus su ni dmigs
6b7('bum): so //de ci'i phyir zhe na/tshe dang ldan pa shar dwa ti'i bu mig gi rnam par shes pa med pa dang /mig gi rnam par shes bstod pa nyid dang /mig gi rnam bar shes pa bden pa dang /mig gi
7a1('bum): @#/ O /rnam par shes pa ngo bo nyid med pa la sngon gyi mtha' mi dmigs/phyi ma'i mtha' mi dmigs/dbus mi dmigs ste//migi gi rnam par 
7a2('bum): shes pa med pa 'ang gzhan ma yin/mig gi rnam par shes pa stong pa nyid kyang gzhan ma yin/mig gi rnam par shes pa dben pa 'ang gzhan ma yin/mgi gi rnam par shes pa ngo bo 
7a3('bum): nyid med pa 'ang gzhan ma yin /byang chub sems dpa' 'ang gzhan ma yin/sngon gyi mtha' 'ang gzhan ma yin/phyi ma'i mtha' 'ang gzhan ma yin/dbus kyang gzhan ma yin no//
7a4('bum): tshe dang ldan pa sha ra dwa ti'i bu de ltar na mig gi rnam par shes pa med pa gang yin pa dang /mig gi rnam par shes pa stong pa nyid gang yin pa dang /mig 'i rnam par shes pa dben ba gang 
7a5('bum): yin pa dang /mig gi rna bar shes pa ngo bo nyid medapa gang yin pa dang /byang chub sems dpa'a' 'ang gzhan ma yin/sdon gyi mtha' gang yin pa dang /phyi ma'i mtha' gad 
7a6('bum): yin pa dang /dbus gang yin pa de thams cad kyang gnyis su med de gnyis su byar med do//tshe dang ldan pa sha ra dwati'i bu rna ba'i rnam par shes pa med pa'i phyir byang chub sems 
7a7('bum): dpa' sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs so//rna ba'i rnam par shes pa stong pa nyid kyi phyir byang chub sems dpa' sngon gyi mthar mi da migs so//rna ba'i rnam par shes pa dben pa'i 
7b1('bum): phyir byang chub sems dpa' sngon gyi mtha' mi dmigs so//rna ba'i rnam par shes pa ngo bo nyid med pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' sngaun gyi mthar mi dmigs so//rna ba'i rnam par shes 
7b2('bum): ba med pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs so//rna ba'i rnam par shes pa stong pa nyid kyi phyir byang chub sems dpa'i phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs so//rna ba'i rnam par
7b3('bum): shes pa dben pa'i phyir byang chub sems dba' phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigasaso//rna ba'i rnam par shes pa ngo bo nyid med pi'i phyir byang chub sems dapa' phyi ma'i mthar mi dmig so//
7b4('bum): rna ba'i rnam par shes pa med pa'i phyir byang chub ses dpa' dbus su mi dmigs so//rna ba'i rnam par shes pa stong pa nyid kyi phyir byang chub sems dpa' dbus su mi dmig so//
7b5('bum): rna ba'irnam par shes pa dben pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' dbus su mi dmigs so//rna ba'i rnam par shes pa ngo bo nyid med pa'i phyir byang chub sems
7b6('bum): de ci'i phyir zhe na/tshe dang ldan pa sha ra dwa ti'i bu rna ba'i rnam par shes pa med pa dang /rna ba'i rnam par shes pa stong pa nyid dang /rna ba'i rnam bar shes pa dben pa dang /rna ba'i rnam par
7b7('bum): shes pa ngo bo nyid med pa la sngon gyi mtha' mi dmigs/phyi ma'i mtha' mi dmigs/dbus mi dmigs te/rna ba'i rnam par shes pa de la 'ang gzhan ma yin/rna ba'i rnam par shes
8a1('bum): @#/ O /pa stong pa nyid kyang gzhan ma yin/rna ba'i rnam par shes pa dben pa 'ang gzhan ma yin/rna ba'i rarnam par shes pa ngo bo nyid med pa 'ang gzhan ma yin/
8a2('bum): byang chub sems dpa' 'ang gzhan ma yin /sngon gyi mtha' 'ang gzhan ma yin/phyi ma'i mtha' 'ang gzhan ma yin /dbus kyang gzhan ma yin no//tshe dang ldan pa sha ra dwa ti'i bu de ltar 
8a3('bum): na rna ba'i rnam par shes pa med pa gang yin pa dang /rna ba'i rnam par shes pas stong pa nyid gang yin pa dang /rna pa'i rnam par shes pa dbenapa gang yin pa dang /rnam ba'i rnam par 
8a4('bum): shes pa ngo bo nyid med pa gang yin pa dang/byang chub sems dpa' gang yin pa dang /sngon gyi mtha' gang yin pa dang /phyi ma'i mtha' gang yin pa dang  /dbus gang yin pa de 
8a5('bum): thams cad kyang gnyis su med de gnyis su byar med do//tshe dang ldan pa shara dwa ti'i bu sna'i rnam par shes pa med ba'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs so//sna'i 
8a6('bum): rnam par shes pa stong pa nyid kyi phyir byang chub sems dpal sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs sau//sna'i rnam par shes pa dben pa'i phyir byang chub sems dadapa' sngon gyi mthar mi dngamigs 
8a7('bum): so|sna'i rnam par shes pa ngo bo nyid med pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' sngon gyi mthar mi dmings so//sna'i rnam par shes pa medapa'i phyir byang chub sems dba' phyi ma'i mthar mi 
8b1('bum): dmigs so//sna'i rnam par shes pa stong ba nyid kyi phyir byang chub sems dpa' phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs so//sna'i rnam pa shes pa dben pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa'i phyi ma'i mthar 
8b2('bum): mi dmigs so//sna'i rnams par shes pa ngo bo nyid med pa'i phyir byang chub sems pa'i phyi ma'i mtha' mi dmigs so//sna'i rnal par shes pa med pa'i phyir byang chub 
8b3('bum): sems dpa' dbus su mi dmigs so//sna'i rnamapar shes pa stong pa nyid kyi phyir byang chub sem dpa' dbus su mi dmig so//sna'i rnam par shesapa dben pa'i phyir byang 
8b4('bum): chub sems dpa' dbus su mi gso so//sna'i rnam par shes//sna'i rnam par shes na ngo bo nyid med pa'i phyir//sna'i rnam par shes pa'i phyir byang chub sems
8b5('bum): rnam par shes pa med pa dang /sna'i rnam par shes pa stong pa nyid dang /sna'i rnam par shes
8b6('bum): pa dben pa dang /sna'i rnam pa shes pa dang /sna'i rnam par//sna'i rnam par shes dben pa dang //sna'i rnam par shes gtan pa ni//sna'i rnam par shes ba dben pa 
8b7('bum): 'ang gzhan ma yin//sna'i rnam par shes pa ngo bo nyid//sna'i rnam par shes pa'ang gzhan ma yin//byang chub sems dpa' 'ang gzhan ma yin//sngon gyi mtha' 'ang gzhan ma yin/phyi ma'i mtha' 'ang
9a1('bum): @#/ O /gzhan ma yin/dbus kyang gzhan ma yin no//tshe dang ldan pa sha ra dwa ti'i bu de ltar na sna'i rnam par shes pa med pa gang yin pa dang /sna'i rnam
9a2('bum): par shes pa stong pa nyid gang yin pa dang /sna'i (  )rnam par shes pa dben pa gang yin pa dang /na'i rnam par shes pa ngo bo nyid med pa gang yin pa dang/byang chub sems dpa' gang yin pa dang /
9a3('bum): sngon gyi mtha' gang yin pa dang /phyi ma'i mtha' gang yin pa dang dbus gang yin pa de thams cad kyang gnyis su med de gnyis su byar med do//tshe dang ldan pa sha ra dra ti'i bu lce'i
9a4('bum): rnam par shes pa med pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs so//lce'i rnam par shes pa stong pa nyid kyi phyir byang chub sems dpa'࿒sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs
9a5('bum): so||lce'i rnam par shes pa dben pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs so//lce'i rnam par shes pa ngo bo nyid medapa'i phyir byang chub sems dba' 
9a6('bum): sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs so//lce'i rnam par shesapa med pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs so//lce'i rnam par shes pa stong pa nyid kyi phyir byang chub seMs 
9a7('bum): dpa' phyi ma'i mthar mi dmi gas so//lce'i rnam par࿒shes pa dben pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' phyi ma'i mthar࿒mi dmigs so//lce'i rna par shes pa ngo bo nyid mer pa'i phyir 
9b1('bum): byang chub sems dpa' phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs so//lce'i rnam par shes pa med pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' dbus su mi dmigs so//lce'i rna par shes pa stong pa nyid kyi phyir 
9b2('bum): byang chub sems dpa' dbusadu mi dmigs so//lce'i rnal par shes pa dben pa'i phyir byang chub semasadapa' dbus su mi dmigasaso//lce'i rnam par shesapa ngo bo nyid med pa'i phyir
9b3('bum): byang chub sems dpa' dbu su mi dmigs so//de ci'i phyir zhe na/tshe dang ldan pa sha ra dwi ti'i bu lce'i rnam par shes pa meng pa dang /lce'i rnam par shes pa stong pa nyidang /lce'i 
9b4('bum): rnam par shes pa dben pa dang /lce'i rnam pa shes pa ngo bo nyid med pa la sngon gyi mtha' mi dmigs/phyi ma'i mtha' mi dmigs/dbus mi dmigs te/lce'i rnam par shes pa
9b5('bum): med pa 'ang gzhan ma yin/lce'i rnam par shes pa steng pa nyid kyang gzhan ma yin/lce'i rna par shes pa dben pa'ang gzhan ma yin/lce'irnam par shes pa ngo bo nyid med da
9b6('bum): 'ang gzhan ma yin/byang chub sems dpa' 'ang gzhan ma yin/sngon gyi mtha' 'ang gzhan ma yin/phyi ma'i mtha' 'ang gzhan ma yin /dbus kyang gzhan ma yin no//tshe dang
9b7('bum): ldan pa sha ra dwa ti'i bu/de ltar na lce'i rnam par shes pa mad pa gang yin pa dang /lce'i rnam shes pa stong pa nyid gang yin pa dang /lce' rnam par shes pa dben gang yin pa dang /
10a1('bum): lce'i rnam par shes pa ngo bo nyid med pa gang yin pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' gang yin pa dang /sngon gyi mtha' gang yin pa dang /phyi ma'i mtha' 
10a2('bum): gang yin pa dang /dbus gang yin pa de thams cad kyang gnyis su med de gnyis su byar med do//tshe dang ldan pa sha ra dwa ti'i bu/lus kyis rnam par shes pa med pa'i phyir/byang chub sems 
10a3('bum): dpa' sngon gyi mtha' mi dmigs so//lus kyi rnam par shes pa stong pa nyid kyi phyir/byang chub sems dpa' sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs so//lus kyi dam par shes pa dben pa'i 
10a4('bum): phyir|byang chub sems dpa' sngonagyi mtha' mi dmigs so/lus kyi rnam par shes pa ngo bo nid med pa'i phyir/byang chub kyi sems dpa' sngon gyi mtha' mi dmi gas so//lus 
10a5('bum): kyi rnam par shes pa med pa' phyir/byang chub sems dang phyi ma'i mtha' mi dmigs so//lus kyi rnam par shes stong pa nyid kyi phyir/byad chub sems dpa' phyi ma'i mthar 
10a6('bum): mi dmigs so//lus kyi rnal par shes pa dben pa'i phyir/byang chub semasadapa' phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs so/lus kyi rnal par shes pa ngo bo nyid med pa'i phyir/byang chu ba 
10a7('bum): sems dpa' phyi ma'i mtha' mi dmigs/lus kyi rnam par shes med pa'i phyir/byang chub sem dpa' dbus su mi dmigs so//lus kyis rnam par shes pa stong pa 
10b1('bum): nyid kyi phyir/byang chub sems dpa' dbus su mi dmigs so//lus kyi rnam par shes pa dben pa'i phyir/byang chub sems dpa'i dbus su mi dmigs so//lus kyi rnam par 
10b2('bum): shes pa ngo bo nyid med pa'i phyir/byang chub sems/dpa' dbus su mi dmigasaso//de ci'i phyir zhe na/tshe dang ldan pa sha ra dwa ti'i bu/lus kyi rnam par shes pa med pa dang /
10b3('bum): lus kyi rnam par shes pa stong pa nyid dang /lus kyi rnam par shes dben pa dang /lus kyi rnam parashes pa ngo bo nyid med pa la sngon gyi mtha' mi dmigs/phyi ma'i mtha' 
10b4('bum): mi dmigs/dbus mi dmigs te/lus kyi rnam par shes med pa 'ang gzhan ma yin/lus kyi rnam par shes stong pa nyid kyang gzhan ma yin/lus kyirnam par shes pa 
10b5('bum): dben pa 'ang gzhan ma yin//lus kyi rnam par shes ngo bo nyid med pa'ang gzhan ma yin/byang chub sems dpa'a'ang gzhan ma yin/sngon gyi mtha' 'ang gzhan ma yin/phyi ma'i 
10b6('bum): mtha' 'ang gzhan ma yin//dbus kyang gzhan ma yin no//tshe dang ldan pa sha ra twa ti'i bu de ltar na lus kyi rnam par shes pa gang yin pa dang /lus kyi rnams par shes pa stong pa nyid
10b7('bum): gang yin pa dang /lus gyi rnam par shes pa dben pa gang yin pa dang //lus kyi rnam par shes pa ngo bo nyid med pa'i gang yin pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' gang yin pa dang /sngon 
11a1('bum): @#/ O /gyi mtha' gang yin pa dang /phyi ma'i mtha' gang yin pa dang /dbus gang yin pa de thams cad kyang Ignyis su med de gnyis su byar med 
11a2('bum): do༐ ༐tshe dang ldan pa sha ra dwa ti'i bu/yid kyi rnam par shes pa med pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs so//yid kyi rnam par shes pa stong pa nyid kyi phyir byang 
11a3('bum): chub sems dpa' sngon gyi thar mi dmigs so//yid gyi rnam par shes pa dben pa'i phyir/byang chub sems dpa' sngon kyi mthar mi dmigs so//yin kyi rnam par shes pa ngo bo nyid 
11a4('bum): med pa'i phyir byang chub sem dpa' sngon gyi mthar mi  dmigs so//yid kyi rnal par shes pa med pa'i phyir/byang chub sem dpa' phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs so//yid kyi rnam 
11a5('bum): par shes pa stong pa nyid kyis phyir byang chub sems dpa'i phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs so//yid kyi rnam par shesapa dben pa'i phyir/byang  chub sems dpa' phyi ma'i mthar mi  dmigs so//
11a6('bum): yid kyi rnam par shes pa ngo bonyid med pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs so//yid kyi rna ma par shes pa med pa'i phyiro/byang chub sems dpa' dbus su mi 
11a7('bum): dmigs so//yid kyi rnam par shes pa stong pa nyid gyi phyir/byang chub sems dba' dbus su mi dmigs so//yid kyi rnam par shes pa dben pa'i phyir/byang chub sems dpa'
11b1('bum): dbus su mi dmigs so//yid kyi rnam par shes pa ngo bo nyid med pa'i phyir/byang chub sems dpa' dbus su mi dmigs so/de ci'i phyir zhe na/tshe dang ldan pa sha ra dwa ti'i bu yid kyi 
11b2('bum): rnam par shes pa med pa dang /yid kyi rnam par shes pa stong pa nyid dang /yid kyi rnam parashes pa dben pa dang /yid kyi rnam pa ra shes pa ngo bo nyid med la sngon gyi mtha' mi 
11b3('bum): dmigs/phyi ma'i mtha' mi dmigs/dbus mi dmigs te/yid kyi rnam par shes med pa 'ang gzhan ma yin/yid kyi rnam par shes stong pa nyid kyang gzhan ma yin/
11b4('bum): yid kyi rnam par shes pa dben pa 'ang gzhan ma yin//yid kyi rnam par shes po ngo bo nyid med pa 'ang gzhan ma yin /byang chub sems dpa' 'ang gzhan ma yin/sngon gyi mtha'
11b5('bum): 'ang gzhan ma yin/phyi ma'i mtha' 'ang gzhan ma yin/dbus kyang gzhan ma yino//tshe dang ldan pa sha ra dwati'i bu de ltar na yid kyi rnam par shes pa med pa gang yin pa dang /
11b6('bum): yid kyi rnam par shes sa stong pa nyid gang yin pa dang /yid kyi rnamapar shes pa dben pa gang yin pa dang /yidakyi rnam par shes pa ngo bo nyid medapa gang yin pa dang /byang chub sems
11b7('bum): dpa' gang yin pa dang /sngon gyi mtha' gang yin pa dang /phyi ma'i mtha' gang yin pa dang/dbus gang yin pa de thams cad kyang gnyis su med de gnyis su byar med do//tshe dang 
12a1('bum): @#/ O /ldan pa sha ra dwa ti'i bu mig gi 'dus te reg pa med pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs so//mig gi 'dus te reg pa 
12a2('bum): stong pa nyid kyi phyir/byang chub sems dpa' sngon kyi mthar mi dmigs so//mig gi 'dus te reg (pa rabar)pa'i phyir byang chub sems (dpa')sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs so//mig gi 
12a3('bum): 'dus te reg pa ngo bo nyid med pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs so//mig gi 'dus te reg pa med pa'i(phyir)byang chub sems dpa' phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs so//
12a4('bum): mig gi 'dus te reg pa stong pa nyid kyi phyir byang chub sems dpa'i phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs so//mgi 'dus te reg pa dben pa'i phyir byang chub sems
12a5('bum): so||mig gi 'dus te reg pa ngo bo nyidamed pa'i phyir/byang chub sems dpa' phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs so//mig gi 'duste reg pa med pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa'
12a6('bum): dbus su mi dmigs so//mig gi+i 'dus te reg pa stong panyid kyi phyir byang chub sems dpa' dbus su mi dmigs so//mig gi 'dus te reg pa dbenapa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa'
12a7('bum): dbus su mi dmigs so/ /mig gi 'dus te reg pa ngo bo nyid med pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' dbus su mi dmigasaso//de ci'i phyir zhe na/tshe dang ldan pa sha ra dwa ti'i bu mig gi 
12b1('bum): 'dus te reg pa med pa dang /mig gi 'dus te reg pa stong pa nyid dang /mig gi 'dus te rig pa dben pa dang /mig gi 'dus ste reg pa ngo bo nyid med pa la sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs/

12b3('bum): te reg pa dben pa 'ang gzhan ma yin//mig gi 'dus te reg pa ngo bo nyid med 'ang gzhan ma yin/byang chub sems dpa' 'ang gzhan ma yin/sngon gyi mtha' 'ang gzhan ma yin/

12b6('bum): gang yin pa dang /sngon gyi mtha' gang yin pa dang /phyi ma'i mtha' gang yin pa dang/dbus gang yin pa de thams cad kyang gnyis su med de gnyis su byar med do//

13a1('bum): @#/ O /sems dpa' sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs so// rna ba'i 'dus te reg pa dben pa'i phyir /byang chub sems dpa' sngon gyi mthar dmigs 
13a2('bum): so||rna ba'i 'dus te reg pa ngo bo nyid med pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' sngon gi mthar mi dmigs so//rna ba'i 'dus te reg pa med pa'i phyir/byang chub sems dpa'
13a3('bum): phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs so//rnaba'i 'dus te reg pa stong pa nyid kyi phyir/byang chub sems dnga' phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs so//rna ba'i 'dus te regapa dben pa'i

13b1('bum): 'dus te de pa dben pa dang /rna ba'i 'dus te reg pa ngo bo nyid med pa la sngon gyi mtha' mi dmigs/phyi ma'i mtha' mi dmigs/dbus mi dmigs te/rna ba'i 'dus te reg med pa 
13b2('bum): 'ang gzhan ma yin/rna ba'i 'dus te reg stod pa nyid kyang gzhan ma yin/rna ba'i mdus te reg pa dben pa'ang gzhan ma yin/rna ba' 'dus te reg pa ngo bo nyad med pa 'ang gzhan

13b4('bum): dwa ti'i bu de ltar na rna ba'i 'dus te reg pa med pa gang yin pa dang /rna ba'i 'du te reg pa stong pa nyid gang yin pa dang /rna pa'i 'dus te reg pa dben pa gang yin pa dang /
13b5('bum): rna ba'i 'dus te reg pa do bo nyid med pa gang yin pa dang/byang chub sems dpa' gang yin pa dang /sngon gyi mtha' gang yin pa dang /phyi ma'i mtha' gang yin pa dang/dbus gang 
13b6('bum): yin pa de thams cad kyang gnyis su med de gnyis su byar med do//tshe dang ldan pa sha radwa ti'i bu sna'i 'dus te reg pa med ba'i phyir/byang chub sems dpa' sngon gyi mtha' mi dmigs 
13b7('bum): so||sna'i 'dus te reg pa stong pa nyid kyi phyir byang chub sems dpa' sngon kyi mthar mi dmigs so//sna'i 'dus te reg pa dbe pa'i phyir byang chub sems dba' sngon gyi mthar mi 
14a1('bum): @#/ O /dmigs so/sni' 'dus te reg pa ngo bo nyid med pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs so//sna'i 'dus te reg pa med pa'i phyir byang chub 
14a2('bum): sems dpa' phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs so//sna'i 'dus te reg pa stong (pa)nyid kyi phyir byang chub sems dpal phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs so//sna'i 'dus te reg pa dben pa'i phyir byang chub sems

14a5('bum): 'dus te reg pa med pa dang /sna'i 'dus te reg pa stang pa nyid dang /sna'i 'dus te regs pa dben pa dang /sna'i 'dus tereg pa ngo bo nyid med pa la sngon gyi mtha' mi dmigs/
14a6('bum): phyi ma'i mtha' mi dmigs/dbus mi dmigs te/sna'i 'dus te reg pa med pa 'ang gzhan ma yin/sna'i 'dus te reg pa stong pa nyid kyang gzhan ma yin/sna'i 'du sa te reg 
14b1('bum): pa dben pa 'ang gzhan ma yin/sna'i 'dus te reg pa ngo bo nyid med pa 'ang gzhan ma yin/byang chub sems dpa' 'ang gzhan ma yin/sngon gyi mtha' 'ang gzhan ma yin/phyi ma'i mtha'

14b3('bum): pa dang /sna'i 'dus te re ga pa dben pa gang yin pa dang /sne'i 'dus te reg pa ngo bo yid med pa gang yin pa dang/byang chub sems dpa' gang yin pa dang /sngon gyi mtha'

14b5('bum): te reg pa med pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs so//lce'i 'dus te reg pa stong ba nyid kyi phyir byang chub sems dpa'i sngon gyi mthar mi dmigasaso//

16a7('bum): nyid gang yin pa dang /lus (  )kyi 'dus te rig pa dben pa gang yin pa dang /lul kyi 'dus te reg pa ngo bonyid med pa gang yin pa dang /(  )byang chub sems dpa' gang yin pa dang /

16b2('bum): yid kyi 'dus te reg pa med pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs so//yid kyi 'dus te reg pa stong pa nyid kyi phyir byang chub sems dpa'i sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs
16b3('bum): so||yid kyi 'dus te reg pa dben pa'i phyir byang chub sems pa'i sngon gyi mthar mi dmig so//yid kyi 'dus te rig pa ngo bo nyid med pa'i phyir byang chub 
16b4('bum): sems dpa' sngon gyi
16b5('bum): mthar mi dmigs so//yidakyi 'dus te reg pa med 'di'i phyir byang chub sems dba' phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs so//yid kyi 'dus te reg pa stod pa nyid kyi phyir byang chub
16b6('bum): ma'i mthar mi dmigs so//yid kyi 'dus te reg dben pa'i phyir byang chung sems dpa' phyi ma'i mthar mi dmig so//yid kyi 'dus te rig pa do bo nyid med pa'i phyir byang
16b7('bum): chub sems dpa' phyi ma'i mtha' mi dmigs so//yid kyis 'dus te reg pa med pa dben pa'i phyir byang
16b8('bum): chub sems
16b9('bum): dpa' dbus su mi dmigs so//yid kyi'adus te reg pa dben 'di'i phyir byang chub sem dpa' dbus su mi dmig so//yid kyis 'dus te/reg pa stong pa nyid kyi phyir byang 
16b10('bum): chub sems
17a1('bum): @#/ O /sems dpa' dbus su mi dmigs so//de ci'i phyir zhe na/tshe dang ldan pa sha ra dwa ti'i bu/yid kyi 'dus te reg pa med pa dang /yid kyi 'dus 
17a2('bum): te reg pa stong pa nyid dang /yid kyi 'dus te reg dben pa dang/yid kyi 'dus te reg pa'i ngo bo nyid med pa la sngon gyi mtha' mi dmigs/phyi ma'i mtha' mi dmigs/dbus mi 
17a3('bum): dmigs/yid kyi 'dus te reg med pa 'ang gzhan ma yin/yid kyi 'dus te reg stong pa nyid kyang gzhan ma yin/yid kyis 'dus te reg pa dben pa 'ang gzhan ma yin/
17a4('bum): yid kyi 'dus te reg pa ngo bo nyid med pa 'ang gzhan ma yin /byang chub sems dpa' 'ang gzhan ma yin/sngon gyi mtha' 'ang gzhan ma yin/phyi ma'i mtha' 'ang gzhan ma yin /
17a5('bum): dbus kyang gzhan ma yino//tshe dang ldan pa sha ra dA ti'i bu de ltar na/yid kyi 'dus te reg ma med pa gang yin pa dang /yid kyi 'duste reg pa stong pa nyid gang yin pa dang /yid
17a6('bum): kyi 'dus te reg pa dbenapa gang yin pa dang /yin kyi 'dus te reg pa ngo ba nyid (med pa)gang yin pa dang /byang chub sems dpa'agang yin pa dang /sngon gyi mtha' gang yin pa dang /
17a7('bum): phyi ma'i mtha' gang yin pa dang /dbus gang yin pa de thams cad kyang gnyis su med de gnyis su byar med do//tshe dang ldan pa sha ra rwa ti'i bu/mig gi 'dus te reg pa'i 
17b1('bum): rkyen gyis tshor ba med pa'i phyir/byang chub sems dpa' sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs so//mig gi 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba stong pa nyid kyi phyir/byang chub sems dnga' sngon 
17b2('bum): gyi mthar mi dmigs so/mig gi 'dus te reg pa'i rten gyis tshor ba dben pa'i phyir/byang chub semasadapa' sngon gyi mthar mi dmi gas so//mig gi 'dus ste reg pa'i rkyen gyis
17b3('bum): tshor ba ngo bo nyid med pa'i phyir/byang chub kyi sems dpa' sngon gyi mtha' mi dmigs so//mig ni 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen kyis tshor ba med pa'i phyir /byang chub sems dpa' phyi ma'i
17b4('bum): mthar mi dmigs so//migag gi 'dus te reg pa'i skyen gyis tshor ba stong pa nyidakyi phyir/byang chub sems pa' phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs so//cig gi 'dus te reg pa'i rken
17b5('bum): gyis tshor ba dben pa'i phir/byang chub sems dpa'-i phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs//mig gi 'dus te regpa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba ngo bo nyid med pa' phyir byang chub sems dpa' phyi
17b6('bum): ma'i mthar mi dmigs so //mig gi 'dus te reg pa' rkyen gyis tshor ba med pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa'i dbus su mi dmigs so//mig gi 'dus te regapa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba
17b7('bum): stong pa nyid kyi phyir byang chub sems dpal dbus su mi dmigs so //mig ni 'dus te regapa'i rkyen gyi tshor ba dben pa'i phyir byang chub semasadapa' dbus su mi dmigs so//mig
18a1('bum): @#/ O /gi 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba ngo bo nyid med pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' dbus su mi dmigs so//de ci'i phyir zhe na/tshe dang ldan pa 
18a2('bum): sha ra dwa ti'i bu mig gi 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyas tshor ba med pa dang /mig gi 'dus te reg pa'i skyen gyis tshor ba stong pa nyid dang /mig gi 'dus te rig pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba dben pa dang /
18a3('bum): mig gi 'dus te reg pa'i rgyen gyis tshor ba ngo bo nyid dang /ma sngon gyi mtha' mi dmigs/phyi ma'i mtha' mi dmigs/dbus mi dmigs te/mig gi 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba 
18a4('bum): med pa 'ang gzhan ma yin/mig gi 'dus te reg pa 'i rkyen gyis tshor ba stong pa nyidakyang gzhan ma yin/mig gis 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyi tshor ba dben pa 'ang gzhan ma yin//mig gi
18a5('bum): 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis/tshor ba ngo bo nyid med pa 'ang gzhan ma yin /byang chub sems dpa' 'ang gzhan ma yin/sngon gyi mtha' 'ang gzhan ma yin/phyi ma'i mtha' 'ang gzhan ma yi na/
18a6('bum): dbus kyang gzhan ma yin no//tshe dang ldan pa sha ra dwa ti'i bu de ltar na mig gi 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba med pa gang yin pa dang /mig gi 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba stong 
18a7('bum): pa nyid gang yin pa dang /mig 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyas tshor ba dben pa gang yin pa dang //mig gi 'dus te reg pa'i rgyen gyis tshor ba ngo bo nyid med pagang yin pa dang /byang chub 
18b1('bum): sems dpa' gang yin pa dang /sngon gyi mtha' gang yin pa dang /phyi ma'i mtha' gang yin pa dang/dbus gang yin pa de thams cad kyang gnyis su med de gnyis su byar med do//
18b2('bum): tshe dang ldan pa sha ra dwa ti'i bu rna ba'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba med pa'i phyir/byang chub sems dpa' sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs so//rna ba'i 'dus te reg pa'i rten gyis tshor ba 
18b3('bum): stong pa nyid kyi phyir/byang chub sems dpa'i sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs sau//rna ba'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba dben pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' sngon kyi mthar mi dmigs so//
18b4('bum): rna ba'i 'dus te reg pa'i rgyen gyis tshor ba ngo bo nyid med pa'i phyir/byang chub sems dpa' sngon gyi mtharami dmigs so//rna ba'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyi tshor ba med pa'i phyir byang chub 
18b5('bum): sems dpa' phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs so//rna ba'i 'dus te reg pa'i
18b6('bum): pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba dbe na pa'i phyir byang chub sem dpa' phyi ma'i mthar mi  dmigs so//rna ba'i 'a dus te reg pa'i rkyen kyis tshor ba ngo bo nyid medapa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' phyi ma'i 
18b7('bum): mthar mi dmigs so//rnaba'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba med pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' dbus su mi dmigs so//rna ba'i bdus te reg pa'i rkyen gyisatshor ba stong pa nyid kyi 
19a1('bum): @#/ O /phyir byang chub sems dpa' dbus su mi dmigs so//rna ba'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba dben pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' 'bus su mi dmigs 
19a2('bum): so||rna ba'i 'dus te reg pa'i rten gyis tshor ba ngo bo nyid med pa'i phyir byang chub sems pa' dbus su mi dmigs so//de ci'i phyir zhe na/tshe dang ldan pa sha ra dwa ti'i bu rna ba'i 'dus
19a3('bum): te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ma med pa dang /rna ba'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba stong pa nyid dang /rna ba'i 'dus te rig pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba dben pa dang /rna ba'i 'dus te reg pa'i

19b1('bum): gang yin pa dang /rna ba'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba dben pa gang yin pa dang /rna ba'-i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gy-is tshor ba ngo bo nyid med pa gang yin pa dang /byang chub

19b3('bum): tshe dang ldan pa sha ra dwa ti'i bu sna'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyenagyis tshor ba med pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs so//sna'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen kyis tshor ba stong

20a1('bum): @#/ O /chub sems dpa' phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs so//sna'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba med pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' dbus 
20a2('bum): su mi dmigs so//sna'i 'du sa te reg pa'i phya rkyen gyis tshor ba stong pa nyid kyi phyir byang chub sems dpa'-i dbus su mi dmigs so//sna'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyi sa tshor ba

20a5('bum): 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyisatshor ba dben pa dang /sna'i 'dus terag pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba ngo bo nyis med pa la sngon gyi mtha' mi dmigs/phyi mtha' mi dmigs/dbus mi dmigs 
20a6('bum): te ༐ sna'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba med pa 'ang gzhan ma yin/sna'i 'dus te reg pa'i rken gyis tshor ba stong pa nyid kyang gzhan ma yin/sna'i 'drus te reg pa'i rkyen

21a6('bum): lce'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyas tshor ba stong pa nyid dang /lce'i 'dus te reg pa'i skyen gyis tshor ba dben pa dang /lce'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba ngo bo nyid med pa la 
21a7('bum): sngon gyi mtha' mi dmigs/phyi ma'i mtha' mi dmigs/dbus mi dmigs te/lce'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba med pa 'ang gzhan ma yin/lce'i 'dus te reg pa' rkyen

22a1('bum): @#/ O /rkyen gyis tshor ba med pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs so//lus kyi 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba stong pa nyid kyi phyir/byang chub sems

22a3('bum): ngo bo nyid med pa'i phyir byang chub sesams dpa' phyi ma'i mthar ming dmigs so//lus kyi 'das te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tsho ra ba med pa'i phyir/byang chub sems dpa' dbus su mi dmigs so//

23a1('bum): @#/ O /so||yid kyi 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba ngo bo nyid med pa'i phyir/byang chub sems dpa' sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs so//yid kyi 'dus te reg 
23a2('bum): pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba med pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs so//yid kyi 'dus te reg pa'i rten gyis tshor ba stong pa nyid kyi phyir/byang chub sems dba' phyi ma'i mtha' 
23a3('bum): mi dmigs so//yid kyi (   )'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyi tshor ba dben pa'i phyir/byang chub semasadapa' phyi ma'i mthar mi dmi gas so//yid kyi 'dus ta reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor pa ngo bo nyid med pa'i phyir 
23a4('bum): byang chub sems dpa' phyi ma 'i mthar mi dmigs so/yid kyi 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba medapa'i phyir/byang chub sems dpa' dbus su mi dmigs so//yid kyis 'dus te reg 
23a5('bum): pa'i rkyenagyis tshor ba stong pa nyid gyi phyir byang chub sems dpa'i dbus su mi dmigs so//yid kyi 'dus te reg  pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba dben pa'i phyir /byang chub sems dpa' dbus su mi

23b2('bum): ma yin/yid kyi 'dus te reg pa 'i rkyen gyis tshor ba stong pa nyid kyang gzhan ma yin/yid kyi 'dus te rig pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba dben pa 'ang gzhan ma yin/yid kyis 'dus te reg pa'i

23b4('bum): gzhan ma yin no//tshe dang ldan pa sha ra dwa ti'i bu de ltar na/yid kyi 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba med pa gang yin pa dang /yid kyi 'dus te reg pa' rkyen gyis tshor ba stong ba nyid 
23b5('bum): gang yin pa dang /yid ki 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gis tshor ba dben pa gang yin pa dang //yid kyi 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba ngo bo nyi med pa gang yin pa dang/byang chub

30a1('bum): @#/ O /ci'i phyir zhe na/tshe dang ldan pa sha ra dwa ti'i bu 'du byed med pa dang /'du byed stong pa nyid dang /'du byed dben pa dang /'du byed ngo bo nyid med pa la sngon 
30a2('bum): gyi mtha' mi dmigs /phyi ma'i mtha' mi dmigs/dbus mi dmigs te/'du byed med pa 'ang gzhan ma yin/'du byed stong pa nyid kyang gzhan ma yin/'du byedadaben pa 'ang gzhan ma 
30a3('bum): yin|'du byed de bo nyid med pa 'ang gzhan ma yid/byang chub sems dpa' 'ang gzhan ma yin/sngon gyi mtha' 'ang gzhan ma yin/phyi ma'i mtha' 'ang gzhan ma yin /dbus kyang 
30a4('bum): gzhan ma yin no//tshe dang ldan pa sha ra dwati'i bu de ltar na 'du byed med pa gang yin pa dang /'du byed stong ba nyid gang yin pa dang /'dud byed ga dben pa gang yin pa dang /'angu byed ngo bo nyid 
30a5('bum): med pa gang yin pa dang  /byang chub sems dpa' gang yin pa dang /sngon gyi mtha' gang yin pa dang /phyi ma'i mtha' gang yin pa dang dbus gang yin pa de thams cad kyang gnyis su 
30a6('bum): med de gnyis su byar med do//tshe dang ldan pa sha radwa ti'i bu rnam par shes pa med pa'i phyir/byang chub sems dpa' sngon gyi mtha' mi dmigs so//rnam par shes pa stong pa nyid kyi phyir༐
30a7('bum): byang chub sems dpa' sngon kyi mthar mi dmigs so//rnam par shes pa dben pa'i phyir/byang chub semasadapa' sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs so//rnam par࿒shes pa ngo bo nyid med pa'i phyir/
30b1('bum): byang chub sems dpa' sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs so//rnam par shes pa med pa'i phyir/byang chub sems dpa' phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs so//rnam par shes pa stong pa nyid kyi phyir/byang 
30b2('bum): chub sems dpa' phyi ma'i mthur mi dmigs so//rnam bar shes pa dben pa'i phyir/byang chub semsdpa' phyi ma'i mthar mi da migs so//rnam par shesapa ngo bo nyid med pa'i phyir/
30b3('bum): byang chub sems dpa' phyi ba'i mthar mi dmigs so/rnam par shes pa med pa'iphyir/byang chub sems dpa'i dbus su mi dmigs so//rnam pa shes pa stong pa nyid kyi phyer/byang 
30b4('bum): chub sems dpa'i dbus su ma dmigs so//rnam par zhes pa dben pa'i phyir//byang chub sems dpa' dbus su mi dmigs so//rnam par shes pa stong pa yid kyi phyir/byang 
30b5('bum): byang chub sems dpa' dbu su mi dmigs so//rnams par shes pa dben pa'i phyi ra/byang chub sems dpa'-i dbus su mi dmigs so/
30b6('bum): shes pa dben pa dang /rnam par shes pa ngo bo nyid med pa la sngon gyi mtha' mi dmigs/phyi ma'i mtha' mi dmigs/dbus mi dmigs te/rnam par shes pa med 'ang gzhan 
30b7('bum): ma yin/rnam par shes pa stong pa nyid kyang gzhan ma yin/rnam par shes par dben pa 'ang gzhan ma yin/rnam pa shes pa ngo bo nyid med 'ang gzhan ma yin/byang chub 
31a1('bum): @#/ O /sems dpa' 'ang gzhan ma yin/sngon gyi mtha' 'ang gzhan ma yin/phyi ma'i mtha' 'ang gzhan ma yin /dbus kyang gzhan ma yin no//tshe dang 
31a2('bum): ldan pa sha ra dwa ti'i bu de ltar na/rnam par shes pa med pa gang yin pa dang /rnam par shes pa stong pa nyid gang yin pa dang /rnam par shes pa (dben pa)gang yin pa dang /rnams par shes 
31a3('bum): pa ngo bo nyid med pa gang yin pa dang/byang chub sems dpa' gang yin pa dang /sngon gyi mtha' gang yin pa dang /phyi ma'i mtha' gang yin pa dang/dbus gang yin pa de 
31a4('bum): thams cad kyang gnyis su med de gnyis su byar med do//tshe dang ldan pa sha ra dwa ti'i bu ming dang gzugs med pa'i phyir/byang chub sems dpa' sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs so//ming 
31a5('bum): dang gzugs stong pa nyid kyi phyir/byang chub sems dpa'i sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs se//ming dang gzugs dben pa'i phyir/byang chub semasadapa' sngon gyi mthar mi dmi gas so//

37a4('bum): cad kyang gnyis su med de gnyis su (gyu) myed do//tshe dang ldan pa sha radwa ti'i bu rga shi med pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs so//rga shi stong pa nyid kyi phyir byang chub (chung)sems dpa' 
37a5('bum): sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs so //rga shi dben pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' sngon gyi mthar mi dmi+igs so//rga shi ngo bo nyid med pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs so//
37a6('bum): rga shi med pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs so//rga shi stong pa nyid kyi phyir byang chub sems dpa' phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs so//rga shi dben pa'i phyir byang chub sems
37a7('bum): dpa' phyi ma'i mthar mi dmi gas so//rga shi ngo bo nying med pa'i phyir byang chub sams dpa' phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs so//rga shi med pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' dbus su mi dmigs so//
37b1('bum): rga shi stong pa nyid kyi phyir byang chub sems dpa' dbus su mi dmigs so//rga shi dben pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa'i dbus su mi dmigs so//rga shi ngo bo nyid med pa'i phyir byang chub sems
37b2('bum): dpa' dbus su mi dmigs sa//de ci'i phyir zhe na/tshe dang ldan pa sha ra dwa ti'i bu rga shi med pa dang /rga shi stong pa nyid dang /rga shi dben pa dang /rga shi ngo bo nyid med pa la sngon gyi mtha'
37b3('bum): mi dmigs/phyi ma'i mtha' mi dmigs/dbus mi dmigs te/rga shi med pa 'ang gzhan ma yin/rga shi stong pa nyid kyang gzhan ma yin/rga shi dben pa 'ang gzhan ma yin//rga

37b7('bum): sha ra dwa ti'i bu/sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa med pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs so//sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin stong pa nyid kyi phyir/byang chub sems dpa' sngon kyi mthar

93b6('bum): phyir|byang chub sems dpa' kun tu mtha' yas par rig par bya zhes zer ba ni/tshe dang ldan pa sha ra dA ti'i bu yid kyi rnam par shes pa ni nam mkha' dang mnyam pa ste/de ci'i 
93b7('bum): phyir zhe na/'di ltar dper na/nam mkha' ni sngon gyi mtha' mi dmigs/phyi ma'i mtha' mi dmigs/dbus mi dmigs te/mtha' yas kun tu mtha' yas pa'i phyir/
94a1('bum): @#/ O /nam mkha' zhes ming du brjod do//tshe dang ldan pa sha ra dwa ti'i bu yid kyi rnam par shes pa stong pa nyid kyi phyir/yid kyi rnam par shes pa'i sngon gyi mtha' mi dmigs/

94a6('bum): pa nyid kyi phyir/mig gi 'dus te reg pa'i sngon gyi mtha' mi dmigs/phyi ma'i mtha' mi dmigs/dbus mi dmi gas te/stong pa nyid la mtha' 'am dbus mi dmigs so//
